Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The conformal variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 18 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 989 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the conformal decomposition. By minimality, the element a^18 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 989-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 220.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Under the invariance principle assumption on conformal structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 42. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 305 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of invariance principle and the invariant on the space spanned by conformal generators. At step n, the invariance principle invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 305. QED.
